  '/**\r\n' +
    ' * Shader injection for the 3D scene.\r\n' +
    ' *\r\n' +
    ' * Everything here is `onBeforeCompile` patches on stock THREE materials rather\r\n' +
    ' * than custom ShaderMaterials. That is deliberate: a raw ShaderMaterial has to\r\n' +
    ' * re-implement lighting, fog and tone mapping by hand, and any drift between it\r\n' +
    ' * and the rest of the scene shows up as an object that looks pasted on. Patching\r\n' +
    ' * MeshStandardMaterial keeps the curve, the floor and the surface under one\r\n' +
    ' * lighting model, so the result reads as a single photograph rather than layers.\r\n' +
    ' *\r\n' +
    ' * It also buys the three headline features almost for free:\r\n' +
    ' *   - the played / unplayed split along the curve is ONE uniform per frame, not\r\n' +
    ' *     a geometry rebuild, so playback costs essentially nothing;\r\n' +
    ' *   - the grid is procedural in the fragment shader, so there is no grid texture\r\n' +
    ' *     to load and the lines stay one pixel wide at every zoom level;\r\n' +
    ' *   - distance fade is computed per fragment against the camera, so the grid and\r\n' +
    ' *     the ground dissolve into the depth of the scene.\r\n' +
    ' */\r\n' +
    'import {\r\n' +
    '  Color,\r\n' +
    '  DoubleSide,\r\n' +
    '  MeshBasicMaterial,\r\n' +
    '  MeshPhysicalMaterial,\r\n' +
    '  MeshStandardMaterial,\r\n' +
    '  type IUniform,\r\n' +
    "} from 'three';\r\n" +
    "import type { ScenePalette } from '../../utils/graph3dTheme';\r\n" +
    '\r\n' +
    '/**\r\n' +
    ' * Anti-aliased grid line.\r\n' +
    ' *\r\n' +
    ' * `fwidth` gives the screen-space derivative, so a line is exactly one pixel\r\n' +
    ' * wide however far away it is ΓÇö the usual failure of hand-rolled grids, where\r\n' +
    ' * distant lines alias into shimmer or vanish entirely.\r\n' +
    ' */\r\n' +
    'const GRID_GLSL = `\r\n' +
    '  float gridLine(float coord, float width) {\r\n' +
    '    float d = abs(fract(coord - 0.5) - 0.5) / fwidth(coord);\r\n' +
    '    return 1.0 - min(d / width, 1.0);\r\n' +
    '  }\r\n' +
    '`;\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '/** World position varying, shared by the floor and surface patches. */\r\n' +

    '\r\n' +
    'export interface CurveUniforms {\r\n' +
    '  uProgress: IUniform<number>;\r\n' +
    '  uPlayed: IUniform<Color>;\r\n' +
    '  uUpcoming: IUniform<Color>;\r\n' +
    '  uAccent: IUniform<Color>;\r\n' +
    '  uHeadGlow: IUniform<number>;\r\n' +
    '  /** Live audio energy, 0..1. Drives a barely-perceptible breath in the stroke. */\r\n' +
    '  uEnergy: IUniform<number>;\r\n' +
    '  /** Stroke width in world units at one unit of distance. */\r\n' +
    '  uWidth: IUniform<number>;\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '/**\r\n' +
    ' * Patches a material so the curve renders as a thin thread of light.\r\n' +
    ' *\r\n' +
    ' * The expansion happens in the VERTEX shader: each vertex is pushed sideways\r\n' +
    ' * along `cross(tangent, viewDirection)`, which is the in-plane direction\r\n' +
    ' * perpendicular to the curve AND perpendicular to the eye. That makes the ribbon\r\n' +
    ' * face the camera from every angle without any CPU work, so orbiting costs\r\n' +
    ' * nothing and the stroke never turns edge-on and disappears.\r\n' +
    ' *\r\n' +
    ' * `uEnergy` widens the stroke by at most a few percent while the equation is\r\n' +
    ' * sounding. That is deliberately small: the brief asks the curve to feel alive,\r\n' +
    ' * not to distort. Anything larger would misrepresent the mathematics, and this\r\n' +
    ' * is a visualization of an equation first.\r\n' +
    ' */\r\n' +
    '/**\r\n' +
    " * Patches the tube's material so playback is visible ON the curve itself.\r\n" +

    ' * Deliberately minimal now. The previous pass drove a flat camera-facing ribbon\r\n' +

    " * falloff. With a physically-based tube, all of that is the geometry's job and\r\n" +
    ' * doing it here would actively fight the lighting: the highlight has to come from\r\n' +
    ' * the real surface normal so it travels convincingly as the camera orbits, which\r\n' +

    'export function applyCurveShader<T extends MeshStandardMaterial | MeshPhysicalMaterial>(\r\n' +
    '  material: T,\r\n' +
    '  uniforms: CurveUniforms,\r\n' +
    '): T {\r\n' +
    '  material.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {\r\n' +
    '    Object.assign(shader.uniforms, uniforms);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader\r\n' +
    '      .replace(\r\n' +
    "        '#include <common>',\r\n" +
    '        `#include <common>\r\n' +
    '        ${WORLD_VARYING}\r\n' +
    '        attribute float positionAlong;\r\n' +
    '        varying float vAlong;`,\r\n' +
    '      )\r\n' +
    "      .replace('#include <begin_vertex>', '#include <begin_vertex>\\n        vAlong = positionAlong;');\r\n" +
    '\r\n' +
    '    shader.fragmentShader = shader.fragmentShader\r\n' +
    '      .replace(\r\n' +
    "        '#include <common>',\r\n" +
    '        `#include <common>\r\n' +
    '        ${WORLD_VARYING}\r\n' +
    '        varying float vAlong;\r\n' +
    '        uniform float uProgress;\r\n' +
    '        uniform vec3 uPlayed;\r\n' +
    '        uniform vec3 uUpcoming;\r\n' +
    '        uniform vec3 uAccent;\r\n' +
    '        uniform float uHeadGlow;`,\r\n' +
    '      )\r\n' +
    '      .replace(\r\n' +
    "        '#include <color_fragment>',\r\n" +
    '        `#include <color_fragment>\r\n' +
    '        // 1.0 where the playhead has passed, 0.0 ahead of it. The not-yet-played\r\n' +
    '        // part of the equation stays present but recessive ΓÇö dimming it to nothing\r\n' +
    '        // would stop the curve reading as a whole object.\r\n' +
    '        float played = smoothstep(uProgress - 0.0015, uProgress + 0.0015, vAlong);\r\n' +
    '        diffuseColor.rgb = mix(uUpcoming, uPlayed, played);`,\r\n' +
    '      )\r\n' +
    '      .replace(\r\n' +
    "        '#include <emissivemap_fragment>',\r\n" +
    '        `#include <emissivemap_fragment>\r\n' +
    '        float playedE = smoothstep(uProgress - 0.0015, uProgress + 0.0015, vAlong);\r\n' +
    '        float headE = exp(-pow((vAlong - uProgress) * 190.0, 2.0));\r\n' +
    '        // Two contributions only: a faint lift along the travelled path, and one\r\n' +
    '        // narrow band at the scanning head. No general glow, no neon.\r\n' +
    '        totalEmissiveRadiance += uAccent * (playedE * 0.08 + headE * 1.2) * uHeadGlow;`,\r\n' +
    '      );\r\n' +
    '  };\r\n' +
    "  material.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'guess-curve-tube-v3';\r\n" +
    '  return material;\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'export interface FloorUniforms {\r\n' +
    '  uMinor: IUniform<Color>;\r\n' +
    '  uMajor: IUniform<Color>;\r\n' +

    'export function applyFloorShader<T extends MeshBasicMaterial | MeshStandardMaterial>(\r\n' +

    '  material.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {\r\n' +
    '    Object.assign(shader.uniforms, uniforms);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader\r\n' +
    "      .replace('#include <common>', `#include <common>\\n${WORLD_VARYING}`)\r\n" +
    '      .replace(\r\n' +
    "        '#include <begin_vertex>',\r\n" +
    "        '#include <begin_vertex>\\n        vWorldPos = (modelMatrix * vec4(transformed, 1.0)).xyz;',\r\n" +
    '      );\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '    shader.fragmentShader = shader.fragmentShader\r\n' +
    '      .replace(\r\n' +
    "        '#include <common>',\r\n" +
    '        `#include <common>\r\n' +
    '        ${WORLD_VARYING}\r\n' +
    '        uniform vec3 uMinor;\r\n' +
    '        uniform vec3 uMajor;\r\n' +
    '        uniform float uMinorOpacity;\r\n' +
    '        uniform float uMajorOpacity;\r\n' +
    '        uniform float uFadeEnd;\r\n' +
    '        uniform vec3 uBase;\r\n' +
    '        uniform float uCell;\r\n' +
    '        ${GRID_GLSL}`,\r\n' +
    '      )\r\n' +
    '      .replace(\r\n' +
    "        '#include <color_fragment>',\r\n" +
    '        `#include <color_fragment>\r\n' +
    '        vec2 g = vWorldPos.xz / uCell;\r\n' +
    '        float minor = max(gridLine(g.x, 1.0), gridLine(g.y, 1.0));\r\n' +
    '        vec2 g5 = g / 5.0;\r\n' +
    '        float major = max(gridLine(g5.x, 1.0), gridLine(g5.y, 1.0));\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '        // Distance from the camera across the ground plane, so the grid thins\r\n' +
    '        // into the scene instead of stopping at a visible edge.\r\n' +
    '        float dist = length(vWorldPos.xz - cameraPosition.xz);\r\n' +
    '        float fade = 1.0 - smoothstep(uFadeEnd * 0.3, uFadeEnd, dist);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '        vec3 gridColor = mix(uMinor, uMajor, major);\r\n' +
    '        float strength = max(minor * uMinorOpacity, major * uMajorOpacity);\r\n' +
    '        diffuseColor.rgb = mix(uBase, gridColor, strength * fade);`,\r\n' +
    '      );\r\n' +
    '  };\r\n' +
    "  material.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'guess-floor-grid-v1';\r\n" +
    '  return material;\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '/* ΓöÇΓöÇ surfaces ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ */\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'export interface SurfaceUniforms {\r\n' +
    '  uContour: IUniform<Color>;\r\n' +
    '  uContourStrength: IUniform<number>;\r\n' +
    '  uContourFreq: IUniform<number>;\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '/**\r\n' +
    ' * Adds contour banding to a surface.\r\n' +
    ' *\r\n' +
    ' * Contour lines are level curves of z, which is genuine mathematical structure\r\n' +

    actual: '/**\r\n * Shader injection for the 3D scene.\r\n *\r\n * Everything here is `onBeforeCompile` patches on stock THREE materials rather\r\n * than custom ShaderMaterials. That is deliberate: a raw ShaderMaterial has to\r\n * re-implement lighting, fog and tone mapping by hand, and any drift between it\r\n * and the rest of the scene shows up as an object that looks pasted on. Patching\r\n * MeshStandardMaterial keeps the curve, the floor and the surface under one\r\n * lighting model, so the result reads as a single photograph rather than layers.\r\n *\r\n * It also buys the three headline features almost for free:\r\n *   - the played / unplayed split along the curve is ONE uniform per frame, not\r\n *     a geometry rebuild, so playback costs essentially nothing;\r\n *   - the grid is procedural in the fragment shader, so there is no grid texture\r\n *     to load and the lines stay one pixel wide at every zoom level;\r\n *   - distance fade is computed per fragment against the camera, so the grid and\r\n *     the ground dissolve into the depth of the scene.\r\n */\r\nimport {\r\n  Color,\r\n  DoubleSide,\r\n  MeshBasicMaterial,\r\n  MeshPhysicalMaterial,\r\n  MeshStandardMaterial,\r\n  type IUniform,\r\n} from \'three\';\r\nimport type { ScenePalette } from \'../../utils/graph3dTheme\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Anti-aliased grid line.\r\n *\r\n * `fwidth` gives the screen-space derivative, so a line is exactly one pixel\r\n * wide however far away it is ΓÇö the usual failure of hand-rolled grids, where\r\n * distant lines alias into shimmer or vanish entirely.\r\n */\r\nconst GRID_GLSL = `\r\n  float gridLine(float coord, float width) {\r\n    float d = abs(fract(coord - 0.5) - 0.5) / fwidth(coord);\r\n    return 1.0 - min(d / width, 1.0);\r\n  }\r\n`;\r\n\r\n/** World position varying, shared by the floor and surface patches. */\r\nconst WORLD_VARYING = \'varying vec3 vWorldPos;\';\r\n\r\n/* ΓöÇΓöÇ the curve: a camera-facing luminous ribbon ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ */\r\n\r\nexport interface CurveUniforms {\r\n  uProgress: IUniform<number>;\r\n  uPlayed: IUniform<Color>;\r\n  uUpcoming: IUniform<Color>;\r\n  uAccent: IUniform<Color>;\r\n  uHeadGlow: IUniform<number>;\r\n  /** Live audio energy, 0..1.     actual: '/**\r\n * Shader injection for the 3D scene.\r\n *\r\n * Everything here is `onBeforeCompile` patches on stock THREE materials rather\r\n * than custom ShaderMaterials. That is deliberate: a raw ShaderMaterial has to\r\n * re-implement lighting, fog and tone mapping by hand, and any drift between it\r\n * and the rest of the scene shows up as an object that looks pasted on. Patching\r\n * MeshStandardMaterial keeps the curve, the floor and the surface under one\r\n * lighting model, so the result reads as a single photograph rather than layers.\r\n *\r\n * It also buys the three headline features almost for free:\r\n *   - the played / unplayed split along the curve is ONE uniform per frame, not\r\n *     a geometry rebuild, so playback costs essentially nothing;\r\n *   - the grid is procedural in the fragment shader, so there is no grid texture\r\n *     to load and the lines stay one pixel wide at every zoom level;\r\n *   - distance fade is computed per fragment against the camera, so the grid and\r\n *     the ground dissolve into the depth of the scene.\r\n */\r\nimport {\r\n  Color,\r\n  DoubleSide,\r\n  MeshBasicMaterial,\r\n  MeshPhysicalMaterial,\r\n  MeshStandardMaterial,\r\n  type IUniform,\r\n} from \'three\';\r\nimport type { ScenePalette } from \'../../utils/graph3dTheme\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Anti-aliased grid line.\r\n *\r\n * `fwidth` gives the screen-space derivative, so a line is exactly one pixel\r\n * wide however far away it is ΓÇö the usual failure of hand-rolled grids, where\r\n * distant lines alias into shimmer or vanish entirely.\r\n */\r\nconst GRID_GLSL = `\r\n  float gridLine(float coord, float width) {\r\n    float d = abs(fract(coord - 0.5) - 0.5) / fwidth(coord);\r\n    return 1.0 - min(d / width, 1.0);\r\n  }\r\n`;\r\n\r\n/** World position varying, shared by the floor and surface patches. */\r\nconst WORLD_VARYING = \'varying vec3 vWorldPos;\';\r\n\r\n/* ΓöÇΓöÇ the curve: a camera-facing luminous ribbon ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ */\r\n\r\nexport interface CurveUniforms {\r\n  uProgress: IUniform<number>;\r\n  uPlayed: IUniform<Color>;\r\n  uUpcoming: IUniform<Color>;\r\n  uAccent: IUniform<Color>;\r\n  uHeadGlow: IUniform<number>;\r\n  /** Live audio energy, 0..1. That makes the ribbon\r\n * face the camera from every angle without any CPU work, so orbiting costs\r\n * nothing and the stroke never turns edge-on and disappears.\r\n *\r\n * `uEnergy` widens the stroke by at most a few percent while the equation is\r\n * sounding. That is deliberately small: the brief asks the curve to feel alive,\r\n * not to distort. Anything larger would misrepresent the mathematics, and this\r\n * is a visualization of an equation first.\r\n */\r\n/**\r\n * Patches the tube\'s material so playback is visible ON the curve itself.\r\n *\r\n * Deliberately minimal now. The previous pass drove a flat camera-facing ribbon\r\n * from a vertex shader that computed its own width, taper and cross-section\r\n * falloff. With a physically-based tube, all of that is the geometry\'s job and\r\n * doing it here would actively fight the lighting: the highlight has to come from\r\n * the real surface normal so it travels convincingly as the camera orbits, which\r\n * is the entire reason the tube is back.\r\n *\r\n * So this patch now only supplies what the geometry cannot know:\r\n *   - the played / upcoming colour split, from the shared playback progress;\r\n *   - a narrow emissive band at the playhead;\r\n *   - a very small energy response.\r\n *\r\n * `positionAlong` is a real attribute on the tube (written by `buildCurveTube`),\r\n * carrying the curve parameter, so it lines up exactly with the samples the audio\r\n * engine is scanning. That is what keeps the 3D playhead locked to the sound.\r\n */\r\nexport function applyCurveShader<T extends MeshStandardMaterial | MeshPhysicalMaterial>(\r\n  material: T,\r\n  uniforms: CurveUniforms,\r\n): T {\r\n  material.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {\r\n    Object.assign(shader.uniforms, uniforms);\r\n\r\n    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader\r\n      .replace(\r\n        \'#include <common>\',\r\n        `#include <common>\r\n        ${WORLD_VARYING}\r\n        attribute float positionAlong;\r\n        varying float vAlong;`,\r\n      )\r\n      .replace(\'#include <begin_vertex>\', \'#include <begin_vertex>\\n        vAlong = positionAlong;\');\r\n\r\n    shader.fragmentShader = shader.fragmentShader\r\n      .replace(\r\n        \'#include <common>\',\r\n        `#include <common>\r\n        ${WORLD_VARYING}\r\n        varying float vAlong;\r\n        uniform float uProgress;\r\n        uniform vec3 uPlayed;\r\n        uniform vec3 uUpcoming;\r\n        uniform vec3 uAccent;\r\n        uniform float uHeadGlow;`,\r\n      )\r\n      .replace(\r\n        \'#include <color_fragment>\',\r\n        `#include <color_fragment>\r\n        // 1.0 where the playhead has passed, 0.0 ahead of it. The not-yet-played\r\n        // part of the equation stays present but recessive ΓÇö dimming it to nothing\r\n        // would stop the curve reading as a whole object.\r\n        float played = smoothstep(uProgress - 0.0015, uProgress + 0.0015, vAlong);\r\n        diffuseColor.rgb = mix(uUpcoming, uPlayed, played);`,\r\n      )\r\n      .replace(\r\n        \'#include <emissivemap_fragment>\',\r\n        `#include <emissivemap_fragment>\r\n        float playedE = smoothstep(uProgress - 0.0015, uProgress + 0.0015, vAlong);\r\n        float headE = exp(-pow((vAlong - uProgress) * 190.0, 2.0));\r\n        // Two contributions only: a faint lift along the travelled path, and one\r\n        // narrow band at the scanning head.     ' *  - Renders only when something changed; pauses when off-screen. Idle cost ~0 GPU.\r\n' +
    ' *\r\n' +
    ' * Playback is read through `getProgress` (called every frame, no React re-renders):\r\n' +
    ' *   getProgress={() => { const s = useStudioStore.getState(); return s.isPlaying ? s.progress : null; }}\r\n' +
    ' * (adjust the two field names to your store). Return null/undefined when idle.\r\n' +
    ' */\r\n' +
    "import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';\r\n" +
    "import * as THREE from 'three';\r\n" +
    "import { computeView, formatTick, type View, type XY } from '../../utils/plotMath';\r\n" +
    "import { resolveBackground, watchTheme, type BgInfo } from '../../utils/themeWatch';\r\n" +
    "import { curve3DPalette, type Curve3DPalette } from '../../utils/curve3dPalette';\r\n" +
    '\r\n' +
    'export interface Curve3DViewerProps {\r\n' +
    '  points: XY[];\r\n' +
    '  label?: string;\r\n' +
    '  getProgress?: () => number | null | undefined;\r\n' +
    "  /** 'none' (default): one finger orbits, page never scrolls. 'pan-y': vertical drags scroll the page\r\n" +
    '   *  and mouse-wheel zoom needs Ctrl/Cmd (so an embedded card never hijacks page scroll). */\r\n' +
    "  touchAction?: 'none' | 'pan-y';\r\n" +
    '  className?: string;\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'const W3 = 8, H3 = 4, D3 = 3; // plot box in scene units\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '// Scene palette lives in utils/curve3dPalette (plain data, tested without a\r\n' +
    '// GPU). Light mode is designed, not inverted: brass curve on a paper floor.\r\n' +
    'type Pal = Curve3DPalette;\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'type SceneApi = {\r\n' +
    '  setPoints(p: XY[]): { ok: boolean; clipped: boolean };\r\n' +
    "  setTouchAction(t: 'none' | 'pan-y'): void;\r\n" +
    '  destroy(): void;\r\n' +
    '};\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    "export default function Curve3DViewer({ points, label, getProgress, touchAction = 'none', className }: Curve3DViewerProps) {\r\n" +
    '  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\r\n' +
    '  const apiRef = useRef<SceneApi | null>(null);\r\n' +
    '  const progressRef = useRef(getProgress);\r\n' +
    '  progressRef.current = getProgress;\r\n' +
    '  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n' +
    '  const [clipped, setClipped] = useState(false);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => {\r\n' +
    '    const mount = mountRef.current!;\r\n' +
    '    try {\r\n' +
    '      apiRef.current = createScene(mount, () => progressRef.current?.(), touchAction, (m) => setProblem(m));\r\n' +
    '    } catch (e) {\r\n' +
    "      console.error('[Curve3DViewer] could not start WebGL', e);\r\n" +
    "      setProblem('3D view unavailable on this device.');\r\n" +
    '    }\r\n' +
    '    return () => { apiRef.current?.destroy(); apiRef.current = null; };\r\n' +
    '    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n' +
    '  }, []);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => {\r\n' +
    '    const api = apiRef.current;\r\n' +
    '    if (!api) return;\r\n' +
    '    try {\r\n' +
    '      const r = api.setPoints(points);\r\n' +
    '      setClipped(r.clipped);\r\n' +
    "      setProblem(r.ok ? null : '3D view unavailable for this equation (nothing drawable in range).');\r\n" +
    '    } catch (e) {\r\n' +
    "      console.error('[Curve3DViewer] build failed', e);\r\n" +
    "      setProblem('3D view unavailable for this equation.');\r\n" +
    '    }\r\n' +
    '  }, [points]);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => { apiRef.current?.setTouchAction(touchAction); }, [touchAction]);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  return (\r\n' +
    "    <div className={className} style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height: '100%', minHeight: 160 }}\r\n" +
    '         role="img" aria-label={label ? `3D view of ${label}` : \'3D graph\'}>\r\n' +
    "      <div ref={mountRef} style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0 }} />\r\n" +
    '      {problem && (\r\n' +
    "        <div style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', textAlign: 'center', padding: 16, fontSize: 13, opacity: 0.8 }}>\r\n" +
    '          {problem}\r\n' +
    '        </div>\r\n' +
    '      )}\r\n' +
    '      {clipped && !problem && (\r\n' +
    "        <div style={{ position: 'absolute', right: 10, bottom: 8, fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.6, pointerEvents: 'none' }}>\r\n" +
    '          y-range trimmed to show the shape\r\n' +
    '        </div>\r\n' +
    '      )}\r\n' +
    '    </div>\r\n' +
    '  );\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'function disposeTree(root: THREE.Object3D) {\r\n' +
    '  root.traverse((o: any) => {\r\n' +
    '    if (!o.isSprite && o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); // sprites share one internal geometry\r\n' +
    '    const mats = o.material ? (Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : [o.material]) : [];\r\n' +
    '    for (const m of mats) { if (m.map) m.map.dispose(); m.dispose(); }\r\n' +
    '  });\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    'function srgb(tex: THREE.Texture) {\r\n' +
    '  const t: any = tex, T: any = THREE;\r\n' +
    "  if ('colorSpace' in t) t.colorSpace = T.SRGBColorSpace; else t.encoding = T.sRGBEncoding;\r\n" +
    '}\r\n' +
    'function rr(g: CanvasRenderingContext2D, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, r: number) {\r\n' +
    '  g.beginPath();\r\n' +
    '  g.moveTo(x + r, y); g.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); g.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);\r\n' +
    '  g.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); g.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); g.closePath();\r\n' +
    '}\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    'function createScene(\r\n' +
    '  mount: HTMLElement,\r\n' +
    '  readProgress: () => number | null | undefined,\r\n' +
    "  initialTouch: 'none' | 'pan-y',\r\n" +
    '  onProblem: (msg: string | null) => void,\r\n' +
    '): SceneApi {\r\n' +
    "  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\r\n" +
    "  canvas.style.cssText = 'display:block;width:100%;height:100%;outline:none;';\r\n" +
    '  canvas.style.touchAction = initialTouch;\r\n' +
    '  mount.appendChild(canvas);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  let renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer;\r\n' +
    '  try {\r\n' +
    "    renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });\r\n" +
    '  } catch (e) { canvas.remove(); throw e; }\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));\r\n' +
    "  renderer.setClearColor(0x000000, 0); // transparent: the card's own background shows through\r\n" +
    '  const ra: any = renderer, T: any = THREE;\r\n' +
    "  if ('outputColorSpace' in ra) ra.outputColorSpace = T.SRGBColorSpace; else ra.outputEncoding = T.sRGBEncoding;\r\n" +
    '  renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\r\n' +
    '  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;\r\n' +
    '  renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  const scene = new THREE.Scene();\r\n' +
    '  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(40, 1, 0.1, 200);\r\n' +
    '  const K = parseInt(THREE.REVISION, 10) >= 155 ? Math.PI : 1; // light units changed in r155\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);\r\n' +
    '  key.position.set(5, 8, 6); key.castShadow = true; key.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); key.shadow.bias = -0.0005;\r\n' +
    '  const sh = key.shadow.camera as THREE.OrthographicCamera;\r\n' +
    '  sh.left = -8; sh.right = 8; sh.top = 8; sh.bottom = -8; sh.near = 1; sh.far = 30;\r\n' +
    '  const fill = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x9db4ff, 1); fill.position.set(-6, 3, -4);\r\n' +
    '  const rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffe2c4, 1); rim.position.set(4, -1, 6);\r\n' +
    '  const amb = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff, 0x445066, 1);\r\n' +
    '  scene.add(key, fill, rim, amb);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  const data = new THREE.Group();\r\n' +
    '  scene.add(data);\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  let points: XY[] = [];\r\n' +
    '  let view: View | null = null;\r\n' +
    '  let bg: BgInfo = resolveBackground(mount);\r\n' +
    '  let segs: { start: number; end: number; curve: THREE.CatmullRomCurve3; bright: THREE.Mesh; ghost: THREE.Mesh }[] = [];\r\n' +
    '  let head: THREE.Mesh | null = null;\r\n' +
    '  const sprites: { s: THREE.Sprite; pxW: number; pxH: number }[] = [];\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  const DEF = { theta: 0.62, phi: 1.18 };\r\n' +
    '  let theta = DEF.theta, tTheta = DEF.theta, phi = DEF.phi, tPhi = DEF.phi;\r\n' +
    '  let radius = 14, tRadius = 14, fitRadius = 14, userZoomed = false;\r\n' +
    '  const target = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\r\n' +
    '  let touchAction = initialTouch;\r\n' +
    '  let needs = true, visible = true, lastP: number | null | undefined = undefined, raf = 0;\r\n' +
    '\r\n' +
    '  function clearData() {\r\n' +
    '    while (data.children.length) { const c = data.children[0]; data.remove(c); disposeTree(c); }\r\n' +
    '    segs = []; head = null; sprites.length = 0;\r\n' +
    '  }\r\n' +
    '  function lines(arr: number[], color: number, opacity: number) {\r\n' +
    '    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\r\n' +
    "    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(arr, 3));\r\n" +
    '    return new THREE.LineSegments(g, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color, transparent: true, opacity, toneMapped: false }));\r\n' +
    '  }\r\n' +
    '  function cone(at: THREE.Vector3, dir: THREE.Vector3, color: number) {\r\n' +

    '    m.position.copy(at);\r\n' +
    '    m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), dir.clone().normalize());\r\n' +
    '    return m;\r\n' +
    '  }\r\n' +
    '  /** Text on a tightly-sized chip, shown at a CONSTANT on-screen size (tiny cards stay readable). */\r\n' +
    "  function label(text: string, pos: THREE.Vector3, kind: 'axis' | 'tick', anchor: [number, number], pal: Pal) {\r\n" +
    "    const texFont = kind === 'axis' ? 44 : 34, screenFont = kind === 'axis' ? 15 : 11;\r\n" +

    "    g.fillStyle = kind === 'axis' ? pal.text : pal.tick;\r\n" +

    '\r\n' +
